   122→- Precedence landmine PROVED empirically: SQLite evaluates A UNION B INTERSECT C left-to-right → 8 ids {1,3,5,6,7,11,13,16}; standard/Postgres/MySQL reading (INTERSECT first) → 15. Also: SQLite rejects parentheses around compound arms ((A UNION B) INTERSECT C and A UNION (B INTERSECT C) both syntax-error) — the portable spelling is FROM-subquery arms (verified parsing).
